# Demo seed v1: the parameter ledger

Generated from `ledger.json` by `checks/build_ledger_md.py`. Do not edit this file: change the row in `checks/build_ledger.py`, run it, run `checks/check_ledger.py`, then run the generator again.

1141 rows. By class: derived 158, designed 954, measured 25, measured-unverified 4. By mark: fixed 712, rate 386, scales 43.

- **Class** says where a value comes from: *measured* (a source that was read), *measured-unverified* (a source still to be read), *derived* (a formula over other rows, recomputed by the check), *designed* (chosen, with its reasoning and range).
- **Mark** says what happens to it when the data is built: *fixed* (a planted instance, the same in both sizes), *scales* (grows with the density of the build), *rate* (a share or a mean, with its tolerance and the number of cases it needs to be readable).
- Where the English and the Russian configuration hold the same value the Russian column says "same". Percentages are per cent numbers. `[key]` in a formula is another row's value.
- Notation: **T**: time.t_rule; **G**: go-live: T - 175 days; **P**: the last price rise: the first Monday on or after T - 150 days; **W0, W-k, W+k**: the ISO week of T, k weeks before, k weeks after; **W90**: (T - 90 days, T]; **P1..P6**: 30-day score periods from G.

## The small size (rule R7)

5 rows.

| Key | English | Russian | Unit | Class | Mark | Stories | Basis: formula, source or reasoning | How the seeded data is checked |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `size.small.density` | 0.25 | same | share of the full size's background events | designed | fixed |  | proposal: size is density (design 0). Background orders, their lines, purchase orders, calls, conversations and background customers are thinned to a quarter, evenly over time. People, shifts, punches, every story and every row marked fixed are the same in both sizes | manifest size; row counts |
| `size.small.scales` | ["volume.*", "calls.per_day.*", "calls.inbound_product_period", "calls.outbound_per_order", "parts.po_per_order", "s2.f1.orders", "s2.f1.cash_orders", "s2.walkin.total", "s6.west_orders", "s6.counts", "s5.rec_jobs_on_days_off", "background customers, cars and conversations"] | same |  | designed | fixed |  | every row marked 'scales' | rows by mark |
| `size.small.fixed` | ["the cast, shifts, punches, requests and no-shows", "every story item: named customers, orders, calls, conversations, cards, notes, pages, meetings, scheduled runs", "acct.*.visits", "s2.f1.discounted and the second and third components", "calls.monday_mornings_since_fix (the seven misses)", "calls.callback.main.after (the two callers)", "calls.queue_at_t", "s6.waiting_at_t", "tax and prices"] | same |  | designed | fixed |  | every row marked 'fixed' | rows by mark |
| `size.small.rate_rule` | a rate row is kept at the small size only if its expected count there reaches its minimum count (check_ledger.py prints the list); otherwise the questions that rest on it are dropped at that size. Rows that mix planted and background events (acct.six.*, s2.f1.discount_share, s2.west.discount_share_*, s6 rates, per-mechanic volume) are full size only | same |  | designed | fixed |  | rule R7: the full size is the eval set; the small size carries the stories and the instance questions | the question table in stories/README.md, column 'small' |
| `size.rate_rule` | rates are dealt, not rolled: for a person and a period the seeder gives the event to round(n x rate) cases. minimum count = the larger of 0.5 / tolerance and 5 / rate (both as fractions; for a rate above 50 % its complement); for a mean score, 8 calls (the product's min_calls_for_rates) | same |  | designed | fixed |  | rule R7; calls/DESIGN.md 'levels are dealt, not rolled' made general | check_ledger.py recomputes every minimum |

| `nf.conv.04` | ["The quote is for SC-4 (front brake pads and rotors, 640.91 at list) and SC-9 (battery, 304.45 at list); it was asked for the owner's sign-off on the Friday of week T-4.", "ERP: the estimate made from this chat is proposed as one of A6's five slow estimates (ledger s1.a6), expired; no order followed.", "The file Sourwood_quote_SC4_SC9.pdf (picture pool 'estimate-pdf').", "SC-4's brakes were quoted once before, after call 22 (the Tuesday of week T-9), and not booked; this quote, front pads and rotors only, is A6's second estimate on SC-4."] | ["Смета — на машины № 7 (Ford Transit: передние диски и колодки, 16 276 по прайсу) и № 8 (Citroën Jumper: аккумулятор, 11 274 по прайсу); нужна к согласованию у директора в пятницу недели T-4.", "ERP: смету из этого чата предлагается считать одной из пяти «медленных» смет A6 (ledger s1.a6), просрочена; заказа не было.", "Файл Smeta_Vkusny_vyezd_Transit_Jumper.pdf (пул 'estimate-pdf').", "Запрос пришёл во вторник в 20:41, после закрытия: так день карточки сохранён, а первая реплика человека приходится на смену слотов 5 и 7.", "Смета звонка 22 (неделя T-9) была на машины № 2 (передние колодки и диски, 16 276) и № 5 (ТО); эта, на № 7 и № 8, — другая: обе входят в пять «медленных» смет A6."] | new facts of story conversation 04: conversations/en/04-s1-web-fleet-quote-waited.json / conversations/ru/04-s1-web-fleet-quote-waited.json | designed | fixed |  | stated by the file itself (its new_facts), accepted in fix pass A as checks/new_facts.json froze them; invented people are in named_customers.json, facts about A1 to A6 in accounts.json | carried by: Chatwoot; the shop-system database; the picture and file pools. checks/check_ledger.py compares the files' new_facts with the snapshot |
| `nf.conv.05` | ["The Monday lists: week T-22: M-103, M-107, M-110, M-114, M-121; week T-15: five vans, M-112 among them; week T-5: M-104, M-106, M-111, M-115, M-119 (M-108 swapped out); week T-4: M-108 added; week W+1: M-102, M-109, M-113, M-116, M-122.", "M-112's front struts and alignment were done on the Monday of week T-15 for 1,119.98 before tax under purchase order 4398A (the 'front struts back in the summer' of story call 25).", "M-110 pulled to the right in week T-22; M-113's heater is weak in week W0; M-122's rear brakes were low at its last service.", "The photo M112_front_strut.jpg (picture pool 'worn-part-strut').", "The contact's picture: a golden retriever looking out of a truck window."] | ["Заявки на понедельник: неделя T-22: Т-02, Т-06, Т-11, Т-15, Т-22; неделя T-5: Т-04, Т-05, Т-13, Т-18, Т-24 (Т-08 заменён); неделя T-4: добавлен Т-08; неделя W+1: Т-03, Т-07, Т-12, Т-16, Т-25.", "Largus Т-09 приехал в понедельник недели T-16 на ТО, у него нашли течь помпы; слот 17 обещал перезвонить с ценой и не перезвонил; помпу заменили в первый рабочий день слота 16 после этого, 12 279,80 по договору, заявка 96/07.", "ГАЗель Т-15 тянуло вправо (неделя T-22); у Granta Т-12 слабо греет печка (неделя W0); у Duster Т-25 задние колодки были на исходе на прошлом ТО; Т-08 в начале октября работал на объекте в Димитровграде.", "Фото T-09_pompa.jpg (пул 'worn-part-water-pump').", "Картинка контакта: герань на подоконнике, за окном двор в снегу."] | new facts of story conversation 05: conversations/en/05-s1-telegram-weekly-vans.json / conversations/ru/05-s1-telegram-weekly-vans.json | designed | fixed |  | stated by the file itself (its new_facts), accepted in fix pass A as checks/new_facts.json froze them; invented people are in named_customers.json, facts about A1 to A6 in accounts.json | carried by: Chatwoot; the shop-system database; the picture and file pools. checks/check_ledger.py compares the files' new_facts with the snapshot |
| `nf.conv.06` | ["The first date, Friday, was given to him when the part was ordered, on the day after the car came in, outside the chat and outside any hand-written call (call 15: 'Fella told me Friday').", "ERP: the purchase order for the angle gear was promised for 7 days before T, then for west's 4th full open day before T; on the day of message 3 the supplier said 'early next week' with no tracking number.", "Chad repeated call 15's promise in writing at 17:21 on the day of the call.", "He has a rental car since 8 days before T.", "The missed call is at 09:12 and call 16 at 14:20, as story call 16 has them; в день третьего сообщения поставщик говорит «на следующей неделе» и не даёт трек-номер.", "Первое сообщение пришло в 20:41, после закрытия, в день смены слота 24; первым ответил слот 23 на следующий день, в свою смену, и чат стал его.", "Во вторник ему нужно было ехать в Набережные Челны; на такси он, по его словам, потратил больше, чем стоит ремонт.", "Голосовое сообщение 0:41 (пул 'voice-stub-ru-3'): в чате на сайте это аудиофайл.", "Пропущенный звонок (10:05) и звонок 16 (15:25) стоят в часы набора звонков; | `nf.conv.08` | ["Patsy Greer, a regular of the Bonny Oaks shop, 2013 Buick Enclave, rear brakes recommended at her last visit; an appointment on the Wednesday of week W+1 at 13:00.", "Angie offers no time of her own inside the week she has asked off (S5): the Wednesday before it with her, or the Friday with Josh (slot 17).", "The contact's picture: a white Buick Enclave under a carport."] | ["Марина Захарова, постоянная клиентка east, Volkswagen Polo 2017 (в образце была Kia Rio 2017: такая же машина в named_customers.json записана на другую Марину того же сервиса), задние колодки рекомендованы на прошлом ТО; запись на понедельник недели W+1, 14:00, принимает слот 16.", "«Принимаю я» сказано про понедельник, её последний рабочий день перед неделей отпуска (S5); на дни внутри этой недели она никого на себя не записывает и вторым временем называет субботу у Артёма (слот 17), его смену.", "Картинка контакта: набережная Волги зимой."] | new facts of story conversation 08: conversations/en/08-s13-telegram-stocktake-regular.json / conversations/ru/08-s13-vk-stocktake-regular.json | designed | fixed |  | stated by the file itself (its new_facts), accepted in fix pass A as checks/new_facts.json froze them; invented people are in named_customers.json, facts about A1 to A6 in accounts.json | carried by: Chatwoot; the shop-system database; the picture and file pools. checks/check_ledger.py compares the files' new_facts with the snapshot |

## People

One row per person in each shop's table; a cell with two values is English / Russian. The class, mark and tolerance of each column are in the table that follows the shops'. Names are `cast.json`'s.

Where a person's base is too small for a rate, the cell is a count of planted instances since G (rule R7): the wrong promises of slots 17 and 22, the dropped customers of slot 22, the callbacks not kept of slots 16 and 29, and slot 7's two wrong promises.
